<h3 style="margin:0.0in;text-indent:0.0in;line-height:normal;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Corporate Title: Executive Director</h3>
<h3 style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 42.55pt;line-height:normal;text-indent:-42.55pt;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Department: Risk Management</h3>
<h3 style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 42.55pt;line-height:normal;text-indent:-42.55pt;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Location: New York                         </h3>
<p style="margin:0.0in;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"> </p>
<p style="margin:0.0in;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"><strong><u>Company overview</u></strong></p>
<p style="margin:0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:#333333;background-color:white">Nomura is a global financial services group with an integrated network spanning over 30 countries and regions. By connecting markets East &amp; West, Nomura services the needs of individuals, institutions, corporates and governments through its three business divisions: Retail, Wholesale (Global Markets and Investment Banking), and Investment Management. Founded in 1925, the firm is built on a tradition of disciplined entrepreneurship, serving clients with creative solutions and considered thought leadership. For further information about Nomura, visit </span><a href="https://www.nomura.com/"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;background-color:white">www.nomura.com</span></a><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:#333333;background-color:white">.</span></p>
<p style="margin:0.0in;text-align:center;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"> </p>
<p style="margin:0.0in;text-align:center;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Aon’s Benefit Index<span style="font-size:8.0pt;color:#202124">®</span>, Nomura’s benefits <strong>rank</strong> <strong>#1</strong> amongst our competitors</p><p style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 6.0pt;text-align:justify;line-height:normal;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Aptos, sans-serif"><strong><u><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Division Overview: </span></u></strong></p>
<p style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 8.0pt;line-height:115%;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Aptos, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:9.0pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Credit Risk Management (CRM) is a key function in protecting the Firm and ensuring prudent risk-taking. CRM is responsible for analyzing counterparty creditworthiness, assigning internal credit ratings, establishing and managing credit risk limits in accordance with the risk tolerance.  In addition, CRM measures and controls credit exposure associated with lending, financing and trading activities. CRM is a “Second Line of Defense” control function.</span></p>
<p style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 8.0pt;line-height:115%;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Aptos, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:9.0pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Within CRM, the Hedge Funds (HF) and Private Equity (PE) credit team is responsible for managing all credit risk involving this large and diverse set of clients. The HF &amp; PE Team sits within the Counterparty Credit Risk silo organizationally and works closely with colleagues in other areas of risk and other functions globally including, Front Office, Legal, Finance, Operations and Collateral Management. </span></p>
<p style="margin:0.0in;line-height:normal;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Aptos, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:9.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">                </span></p>
<p style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 6.0pt;text-align:justify;line-height:normal;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Aptos, sans-serif"><strong><u><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Role Description:</span></u></strong></p>
<p style="margin:0.0in;line-height:115%;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Aptos, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:9.0pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">The candidate will be responsible for independently managing and monitoring exposure to a diverse portfolio of fund counterparties generated by activities including OTC derivatives, securities financing, prime brokerage, loan agreements, and other structured or bespoke transactions. The role requires strong technical expertise in hedge fund and private equity fund credit risk, proven stakeholder management skills, and the ability to lead and develop a high-performing team.</span></p>
<p style="margin:0.0in;line-height:115%;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Aptos, sans-serif"> </p>
<p style="margin:0.0in;line-height:115%;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Aptos, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:9.0pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Key Responsibilities</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top:0.0in;margin-bottom:0.0in">
<li style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;line-height:normal;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Aptos, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:9.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Lead and manage a team within the Hedge Funds and Private Equity Funds Credit Risk team in New York, providing day-to-day oversight and guidance to a team of VPs, Associates and Analysts </span></li>
<li style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;line-height:normal;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Aptos, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:9.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Review and approve credit memos, ratings and limits under delegated authority; assess creditworthiness of hedge funds and private equity funds through comprehensive analysis of fund strategy, performance attribution, liquidity profile, leverage, and manager or sponsor strength</span></li>
<li style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;line-height:normal;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Aptos, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:9.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Conduct due diligence visits and calls with HF and PE clients; analyze trading strategies, risk management frameworks, performance metrics, operational controls and governance structures to produce high-quality credit assessments</span></li>
<li style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;line-height:normal;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Aptos, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:9.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Collaborate with Front Office Risk and other risk functions to understand stress loss scenarios; perform ongoing monitoring including monthly/quarterly NAV reviews, performance attribution analysis, and proactive identification of deteriorating credit quality; review credit limit breaches and recommend appropriate credit actions when risk appetite thresholds are exceeded</span></li>
<li style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;line-height:normal;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Aptos, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:9.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Review and approve trading activity, margin requirements and structural enhancements to transactions; oversee daily trade approvals in coordination with other risk teams and establish appropriate margining requirements</span></li>
<li style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;line-height:normal;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Aptos, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:9.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Define credit terms and covenants for legal documentation including Prime Brokerage agreements, ISDA/CSA, GMRA, and negotiate legal documentation while maintaining thorough understanding of associated covenants</span></li>
<li style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;line-height:normal;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Aptos, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:9.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Maintain close communication with HF and PE team leads globally and monitor international risk developments; present comprehensive Americas portfolio risk summaries at global risk review calls and regional/global risk committees</span></li>
<li style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;line-height:normal;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Aptos, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:9.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Establish and maintain constructive relationships with Front Office, Market Risk, Legal, Operations, Compliance and Finance; oversee client engagement and credit standards in coordination with key firmwide stakeholders</span></li>
<li style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;line-height:normal;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Aptos, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:9.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Support enhancement of specialized data analytics and portfolio monitoring tools; lead or participate in firmwide projects such as credit policy development, system implementations, and regulatory</span></li>
</ul>
<p style="margin:0.0in;line-height:130%;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Aptos, sans-serif"> </p>
<p style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 6.0pt;text-align:justify;line-height:normal;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Aptos, sans-serif"><strong><u><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Skills, experience, qualifications and knowledge required:</span></u></strong></p>
<p style="margin:0.0in;line-height:130%;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Aptos, sans-serif"> </p>
<ul style="margin-top:0.0in;margin-bottom:0.0in">
<li style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;line-height:normal;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Aptos, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:9.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">12+ years of relevant experience in managing credit risk to Hedge Funds and/or Private Equity Funds at a major financial institution, with proven team management experience including application of delegated approval authority</span></li>
<li style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;line-height:normal;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Aptos, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:9.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Deep understanding of alternative investment strategies and well-versed in leading client due diligence meetings; experience in both hedge funds and private equity transactions/structures strongly preferred</span></li>
<li style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;line-height:normal;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Aptos, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:9.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Strong knowledge of all major derivative instruments and Prime Brokerage products/services with full understanding of their respective risk characteristics; thorough understanding of master trading documentation (ISDA, CSA, GMRA, F&amp;O, Prime Brokerage agreements)</span></li>
<li style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;line-height:normal;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Aptos, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:9.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Strong quantitative skills with ability to interpret complex risk metrics for informed credit decision-making; comprehensive understanding of portfolio trade risks and credit risk frameworks</span></li>
<li style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;line-height:normal;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Aptos, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:9.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Proven ability to lead, develop and mentor analysts in a fast-paced, high-pressure environment with ability to prioritize and complete tasks according to project needs</span></li>
<li style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;line-height:normal;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Aptos, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:9.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Excellent interpersonal and communication skills with demonstrated ability to engage effectively with Sales teams, Front Office, and external clients at all levels; strong negotiation and consensus-building abilities</span></li>
<li style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;line-height:normal;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Aptos, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:9.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Experience working closely with various business partners such as sales/trading, market risk, legal, operations and client integration; demonstrated business and project management acumen with executive presence</span></li>
</ul>
<p><span style="font-size:9.0pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Bachelor's degree in Finance, Economics, Mathematics, or related field required; advanced degree</span></p><p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><strong><u><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Nomura Competencies</span></u></strong></p>

By connecting markets East &amp; West, Nomura services the needs of individuals, institutions, corporates and governments through its three business divisions: Wealth Management, Investment Management, and Wholesale (Global Markets and Investment Banking). Founded in 1925, the firm is built on a tradition of disciplined entrepreneurship, serving clients with creative solutions and considered thought leadership. For further information about Nomura, visit <a href="http://www.nomura.com">www.nomura.com</a><br> <br>Nomura Services India, (Powai) supports Nomura’s businesses around the world. Powai’ s world class capabilities in trading support, research, information technology, financial control, operations, risk management and legal support have played a key role in facilitating Nomura’s global operations and are an integral part of Nomura’s global expansion plans. The Powai operation is a critical part of the platform to support the growth of Nomura’s global business.</p><p style="margin:11.05pt 0.0in 0.0in 6.6pt;background-color:#bfbfbf;line-height:115%;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><strong><span style="font-size:8.5pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Business Unit Overview: </span></strong></p>
<p style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 6.6pt;text-align:justify;line-height:115%;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"> </p>
<p style="margin:1.4pt 7.75pt 0.0in 6.6pt;text-align:justify;line-height:115%;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:8.5pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Investment Banking Powai team is an extension of the regional Investment Banking (Front Office) teams supporting the following key areas: </span></p>
<ul style="margin-top:1.4pt;margin-bottom:0.0in">
<li style="margin:1.4pt 7.75pt 0.0in 0.0px;text-align:justify;line-height:115%;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:8.5pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">M&amp;A</span></li>
<li style="margin:1.4pt 7.75pt 0.0in 0.0px;text-align:justify;line-height:115%;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:8.5pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Equity Advisory &amp; Private Placements</span></li>
<li style="margin:1.4pt 7.75pt 0.0in 0.0px;text-align:justify;line-height:115%;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:8.5pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Secondary Advisory, Debt Advisory</span></li>
<li style="margin:1.4pt 7.75pt 0.0in 0.0px;text-align:justify;line-height:115%;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:8.5pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Coverage / Sector Banking </span></li>
<li style="margin:1.4pt 7.75pt 0.0in 0.0px;text-align:justify;line-height:115%;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:8.5pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Debt Capital Markets</span></li>
<li style="margin:1.4pt 7.75pt 0.0in 0.0px;text-align:justify;line-height:115%;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:8.5pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Acquisition Leveraged Finance</span></li>
<li style="margin:1.4pt 7.75pt 0.0in 0.0px;text-align:justify;line-height:115%;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:8.5pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Debt Syndicate</span></li>

<li style="margin:1.4pt 7.75pt 0.0in 0.0px;text-align:justify;line-height:115%;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:8.5pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Japanese Equity Syndicate (Equities &amp; Equity Linked Bonds)</span></li>
</ul>
<p style="margin:1.4pt 7.75pt 0.0in 26.0pt;text-align:justify;line-height:115%;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"> </p>
<p style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 6.0pt 4.0pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:4.0pt;line-height:115%;background-color:#bfbfbf;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><strong><span style="font-size:8.5pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Role Description               </span></strong></p>
<p style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 10.0pt 12.0pt;text-align:justify;line-height:115%;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:8.5pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">This role is within the International IB Risk and Control Team. </span><span style="font-size:8.5pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">The role will be responsible for supporting the Head of IBCO, International in developing and operating the risk and control environment in Investment Banking across EMEA &amp; AEJ regions. In particular:</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top:1.4pt;margin-bottom:10.0pt">
<li style="margin:1.4pt 7.75pt 10.0pt 0.0px;text-align:justify;line-height:115%;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:8.5pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Support IB Supervisors in implementing and operating the IB Supervision Framework. </span></li>
<li style="margin:1.4pt 7.75pt 10.0pt 0.0px;text-align:justify;line-height:115%;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:8.5pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Implement and maintain the IB Key Supervisory Controls (KSC). </span></li>
<li style="margin:1.4pt 7.75pt 10.0pt 0.0px;text-align:justify;line-height:115%;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:8.5pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Perform banking controls as required, including KYC Salesforce control and Adverse News. </span></li>
<li style="margin:1.4pt 7.75pt 10.0pt 0.0px;text-align:justify;line-height:115%;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:8.5pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Review Data Loss Prevention (DLP) alerts from a first-line perspective. </span></li>
<li style="margin:1.4pt 7.75pt 10.0pt 0.0px;text-align:justify;line-height:115%;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:8.5pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Implement conduct risk frameworks in line with regional requirements. </span></li>
<li style="margin:1.4pt 7.75pt 10.0pt 0.0px;text-align:justify;line-height:115%;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:8.5pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Investigate conduct events and incidents as required. </span></li>
<li style="margin:1.4pt 7.75pt 10.0pt 0.0px;text-align:justify;line-height:115%;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:8.5pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Support Global / Wholesale reporting and MI as required. </span></li>
<li style="margin:1.4pt 7.75pt 10.0pt 0.0px;text-align:justify;line-height:115%;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:8.5pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Manage the IB new joiner, transfer, and Supervisor processes, including associated training. </span></li>
<li style="margin:1.4pt 7.75pt 10.0pt 0.0px;text-align:justify;line-height:115%;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:8.5pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Review and maintain the IB Conflicts Registers. </span></li>
<li style="margin:1.4pt 7.75pt 10.0pt 0.0px;text-align:justify;line-height:115%;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:8.5pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Support the cross-border frameworks — ranging from travel to Hong Kong (temporary licensing), Singapore travel and the FRC framework, UK cross-border interactions, and US 15a-6 requirements. </span></li>
<li style="margin:1.4pt 7.75pt 10.0pt 0.0px;text-align:justify;line-height:115%;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:8.5pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Maintain a training and licensing requirements register / tool. </span></li>
<li style="margin:1.4pt 7.75pt 10.0pt 0.0px;text-align:justify;line-height:115%;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:8.5pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Support the review and drafting of IBD Policies and Procedures and the IB Supervision Handbook. </span></li>
<li style="margin:1.4pt 7.75pt 10.0pt 0.0px;text-align:justify;line-height:115%;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:8.5pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Coordinate with IT / Development teams to enhance and automate the supervision framework — including, but not limited to, personal and compliance conflict-of-interest reporting, registrations, and supervision reports. </span></li>
<li style="margin:1.4pt 7.75pt 10.0pt 0.0px;text-align:justify;line-height:115%;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:8.5pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Collate information from stakeholders and prepare meeting packs, MI, and minutes for IB governance forums and committees. </span></li>
<li style="margin:1.4pt 7.75pt 10.0pt 0.0px;text-align:justify;line-height:115%;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:8.5pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Support the function in collating information and drafting responses to Audit, Compliance, and external reviews. </span></li>
<li style="margin:1.4pt 7.75pt 0.0in 0.0px;text-align:justify;line-height:115%;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:8.5pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Provide support to non-financial risk programs, including regulatory and business change globally, as required.</span></li>
</ul>
<p style="margin:0.0in;line-height:115%;background-color:#bfbfbf;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><strong><span style="font-size:8.5pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Skills, experience, qualifications and knowledge required</span></strong></p>
<p style="margin:0.0in;line-height:115%;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><strong><u><span style="font-size:8.5pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"> </span></u></strong></p>
<ul style="margin-top:1.4pt;margin-bottom:10.0pt">
<li style="margin:1.4pt 7.75pt 10.0pt 0.0px;text-align:justify;line-height:115%;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:8.5pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Strong experience in Investment Banking, with a solid understanding of the product spectrum and IB operations. </span></li>
<li style="margin:1.4pt 7.75pt 10.0pt 0.0px;text-align:justify;line-height:115%;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:8.5pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Proven experience operating a risk and control environment within a large front-office business. </span></li>
<li style="margin:1.4pt 7.75pt 10.0pt 0.0px;text-align:justify;line-height:115%;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:8.5pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Ability to engage with, and deliver to, stakeholders across the Investment Banking business. </span></li>
<li style="margin:1.4pt 7.75pt 10.0pt 0.0px;text-align:justify;line-height:115%;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:8.5pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Ability to project-manage multiple department-wide projects and initiatives concurrently. </span></li>
<li style="margin:1.4pt 7.75pt 10.0pt 0.0px;text-align:justify;line-height:115%;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:8.5pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Strong interpersonal skills, with the ability to operate at all levels of the business and across the Wholesale division. </span></li>
<li style="margin:1.4pt 7.75pt 10.0pt 0.0px;text-align:justify;line-height:115%;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:8.5pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Ability to build and maintain a network of relevant stakeholders across Banking and Corporate functions. </span></li>
<li style="margin:1.4pt 7.75pt 10.0pt 0.0px;text-align:justify;line-height:115%;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:8.5pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Adaptability to change, with the ability to build relationships and collaborate effectively. </span></li>
<li style="margin:1.4pt 7.75pt 0.0in 0.0px;text-align:justify;line-height:115%;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:8.5pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Organized, methodical approach to work, with the ability to present complex subjects in an accessible way.</span></li>
<li style="margin:1.4pt 7.75pt 0.0in 0.0px;text-align:justify;line-height:115%;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:8.5pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Strong, independent judgment with the confidence to constructively challenge senior front-office stakeholders while maintaining objectivity and a clear escalation mindset.</span></li>
<li style="margin:1.4pt 7.75pt 0.0in 0.0px;text-align:justify;line-height:115%;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:8.5pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Excellent written skills, with the ability to draft and review policies, procedures, handbooks, investigation findings, and governance MI to a high standard.</span></li>
<li style="margin:1.4pt 7.75pt 0.0in 0.0px;text-align:justify;line-height:115%;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:8.5pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Openness to leveraging emerging technologies, including AI and automation tools, to enhance the efficiency and effectiveness of supervisory controls, MI, and reporting.  </span></li>
</ul>

<p style="text-align:justify;line-height:115%;margin:0.0cm;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><strong><span style="font-size:10.0pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"> </span></strong></p>
<p style="text-align:justify;line-height:115%;margin:0.0cm;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Nomura is an Asia-headquartered financial services group with an integrated global network spanning over 30 countries. By connecting markets East &amp; West, Nomura services the needs of individuals, institutions, corporates and governments through its four business divisions: Retail, Asset Management, Wholesale (Global Markets and Investment Banking), and Merchant Banking. Founded in 1925, the firm is built on a tradition of disciplined entrepreneurship, serving clients with creative solutions and considered thought leadership. For further information about Nomura, visit </span><a href="http://www.nomura.com"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:red">www.nomura.com</span></a></p>
<p style="text-align:justify;line-height:115%;margin:0.0cm;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"> </p>
<p style="text-align:justify;line-height:115%;margin:0.0cm;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Nomura Services India, (Powai) supports Nomura’s businesses around the world. Powai’ s world class capabilities in trading support, research, information technology, financial control, operations, risk management and legal support have played a key role in facilitating Nomura’s global operations and are an integral part of Nomura’s global expansion plans. The Powai operation is a critical part of the platform to support the growth of Nomura’s global business. </span></p>
<p style="text-align:justify;line-height:115%;margin:0.0cm;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><strong><span style="font-size:10.0pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"> </span></strong></p>
<p style="margin:0.0cm;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black"><strong><span style="font-size:10.0pt">Division Overview:</span></strong></p>
<p style="margin:0.0cm;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black"><strong><span style="font-size:10.0pt"> </span></strong></p>
<p style="margin:0.0cm;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black"><span style="font-size:10.0pt">Global Markets Division: Based in Mumbai, the front-office team which works on a team extension model delivers pricing, modelling and risk management expertise to the Global Markets business. Founded as a centre of excellence, the group leads the way in price discovery and portfolio optimization techniques and is integral to the business’ aggressive revenue targets. The team is playing an integral role in structuring, pricing, risk management and new idea generation.</span></p>
<p style="margin:0.0cm;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"> </p>
<p style="margin:0.0cm;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><strong><span style="font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Team Overview</span></strong></p>
<p style="margin:0.0cm;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"> </p>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0cm;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">The Fixed Income Electronic Markets team is responsible for managing Nomura’s Rates and Credit Electronic trading offering. This primarily means overseeing client distribution, development of the electronic platform and supporting electronic trading activity. The team supports businesses globally and there is heavy interaction with sales, trading, technology, and external clients/vendors.</span></p>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0cm;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><strong><span style="font-size:10.5pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black"> </span></strong></p>
<p style="margin:0.0cm;text-align:justify;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">By developing strong relationships with our client base through consistent interaction, independent advice and pre-eminent access to Asia, we have built a powerful global franchise. </span></p>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0cm;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"> </p>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0cm;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">The Electronic Trading Automation team is responsible for supporting Nomura’s Fixed Income electronic trading offering to clients. This role primarily focuses on developing and maintaining automated trading solutions, improving operational efficiency, and reducing manual intervention in client onboarding processes. It combines machine learning expertise with advanced automation to develop cutting-edge trading solutions in our state-of-the-art Data Labs environment.</span></p>

<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><strong><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Division:</span></strong><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">                      Finance</span></p>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><strong><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Location:</span></strong><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">                     Singapore</span></p>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><strong><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">SFID:</span></strong><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">                            15043</span></p>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><strong><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Licensed Role: </span></strong><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">           No</span></p>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"> </p>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Helvetica N Roman&#39;;color:black"><strong><u><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:windowtext">Nomura Overview </span></u></strong></p>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Helvetica N Roman&#39;;color:black"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;background-color:white">Nomura is a financial services group with an integrated global network. By connecting markets East &amp; West, we service the needs of individuals, institutions, corporates and governments through our four business divisions: Wealth Management, Investment Management, Wholesale (Global Markets and Investment Banking) and Banking. </span></p>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Helvetica N Roman&#39;;color:black"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;background-color:white">  </span></p>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Helvetica N Roman&#39;;color:black"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;background-color:white">Driven by the insights of some 28,000 people worldwide, we put our clients at the center of everything we do, delivering unparalleled access to, from and within Asia. For further information about Nomura, visit </span><a href="https://www.nomura.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;background-color:white">www.nomura.com</span></a><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;background-color:white">  </span></p>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Helvetica N Roman&#39;;color:black"> </p><p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Helvetica N Roman&#39;;color:black"><strong><u><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:windowtext">Department Overview</span></u></strong></p>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Nomura's Finance department manages the company's financial operations and provides financial guidance to support business decision-making. The team collaborates with senior leadership, business units, and stakeholders to develop effective financial strategies, providing guidance on financial planning, budgeting, forecasting, and analysis to align financial operations with business objectives. </span></p>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"> </p>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black;background-color:white">Treasury is an established function within the Finance division at Nomura. The department has regional teams in Japan, AEJ, EMEA and the US. The team is responsible for all aspects of liquidity and funding requirements of the firm in accordance with the Executive Management Boards mandated risk appetite and in compliance with regulatory liquidity metrics. </span></p>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Helvetica N Roman&#39;;color:black"> </p>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Helvetica N Roman&#39;;color:black"><strong><u><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:windowtext">Role Description</span></u></strong></p>
<p style="text-align:justify;line-height:115%;margin:0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">The Treasury Business Finance Control (BFC) team looks after trade support and revenue reporting, control and governance based in Singapore.  Product coverage includes vanilla Fixed Income and FX products and internal structured funding structures. The team within BFC at Nomura acts as a key advisory and value-add function to the business covering various aspects of finance (eg. Treasury Funding charges, Unsecured Funding, Issuance funding booking model, FX sell down, legal entity booking model, business analysis, issuance-entity related projects, financial resource optimization, cross-functional liaison etc.) </span></p>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Helvetica N Roman&#39;;color:black"> </p>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Helvetica N Roman&#39;;color:black"><u><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:windowtext">Responsibilities</span></u></p>
<p style="margin:0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"> </p>
<p style="margin:0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><em><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">P&amp;L, Balance Sheet and Legal Entity Control</span></em></p>
<ul style="margin-bottom:0.0in;margin-top:0.0px">
<li style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Own the overall integrity of daily/monthly P&amp;L for Treasury &amp; Corporate books, providing senior review and sign-off on revenue attribution across key drivers (funding spread, interest rate risk, FX translation, liquidity premium).</span></li>
<li style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Take ownership of significant P&amp;L and BS movements across all regional entities feeding into overall Global Treasury pool: maintain sources and uses of funds for the regional entities, and act as the primary escalation point to Funding Desk Head. </span></li>
<li style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Oversee regional issuance entity from Treasury PC perspective, ensuring controls are consistently applied and enhanced during key reporting periods (month-end, quarter-end); identify gaps and drive remediation where control breaches or near-misses occur.</span></li>
<li style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Provide support to other business line controllers as the SPOC on funding allocation and new FTP going live working closely with Treasury planning team. Implement appropriate accounting in legal entities as required (e.g. hedge accounting)</span></li>
</ul>
<p style="margin:0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"> </p>
<p style="margin:0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><em><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Governance, Policy &amp; Controls</span></em></p>
<ul style="margin-bottom:0.0in;margin-top:0.0px">
<li style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Support book management processes including book opening/closing, dormancy reviews for Treasury and Corporate books. </span></li>
<li style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Participate in Trading Book / Banking Book classification reviews, ensuring appropriate approvals (Reg Cap, PC, Market Risk) are obtained and documented ahead of any reclassification.</span></li>
<li style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Maintain robust documentation trails for governance decisions to support audit and regulatory review.</span></li>
<li style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Investigate and resolve breaks (e.g., P&amp;L and control account/book breaks), identifying root causes, coordinating with Middle Office/Settlements, and escalating governance gaps to the appropriate action owner.</span></li>
<li style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Ensure timely escalation of outstanding breaks to Senior Management and Business per control standards.</span></li>
</ul>
<p style="margin:0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"> </p>
<p style="margin:0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><em><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Business Partnership &amp; Strategy</span></em></p>
<ul style="margin-bottom:0.0in;margin-top:0.0px">
<li style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Partner directly with Treasury Desk Heads to identify and drive opportunities for funding cost optimization.</span></li>
<li style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Enhance funding-related analytics in partnership with Global Markets (GM), developing improved attribution and insight into funding cost drivers, cross-desk funding usage, and cost allocation methodologies to support desk-level decision-making.</span></li>
<li style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Lead the design and enhancement of Unsecured Funding (UF) reporting, improving transparency, granularity, and consistency of UF metrics presented to senior stakeholders.</span></li>
<li style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Act as a trusted advisor to Treasury and GM stakeholders on the P&amp;L and balance sheet implications of proposed funding structures within the region. </span></li>
<li style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Identify opportunities to streamline or automate recurring funding and UF reporting processes, reducing manual/best-efforts workarounds where scalable solutions can be developed.</span></li>
</ul>
<p style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 4.85pt;text-indent:0.0in;line-height:normal;font-size:9.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"> </p>
<p style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 4.85pt;text-indent:0.0in;line-height:normal;font-size:9.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"><em><span style="font-size:10.0pt">Team Leadership</span></em></p>
<ul style="margin-bottom:0.0in;margin-top:0.0px">
<li style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Develop a team of finance professionals in both on and offshore</span></li>
<li style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Foster a culture of continuous improvement and professional development</span></li>
<li style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Collaborate cross-functionally with Treasury, Regulatory Reporting, Risk, Operations, Middle Office and Technology teams</span></li>
<li style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Mentor junior team members and support their career growth</span></li>
</ul>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Helvetica N Roman&#39;;color:black"> </p>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Helvetica N Roman&#39;;color:black"><strong><u><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:windowtext">Requirements</span></u></strong></p>
<ul style="margin-top:0.0in;margin-bottom:0.0in" type="disc">
<li style="margin-right:2.05pt;margin-top:0.0in;margin-bottom:0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Minimum Degree in Accounting, Economics or related field</span></li>
</ul>
<ul style="margin-bottom:0.0in;margin-top:0.0px">
<li style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Minimum 8 years of experience in a Product Control function supporting Treasury or Fixed Income businesses, including at least 3 years of experience managing and developing teams.</span></li>
<li style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Minimum 8 years of experience in treasury and money market products, with demonstrated experience in repos, deposits, FX swaps, interest rate derivatives, internal funding /hedging structures.</span></li>
<li style="line-height:115%;margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Experience in P&amp;L attribution, FX Exposure, FX Sell down, standard funding charges, cross border booking and controls </span></li>
<li style="line-height:115%;margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Strong understanding of Accounting ledger entries (debit/credit) and technical accounting (like hedge accounting and hedge effectiveness)</span></li>
<li style="line-height:115%;margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Proven track record of partnering with business leaders and influencing strategic decisions</span></li>
<li style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Excellent analytical and problem-solving abilities.</span></li>
<li style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Strong attention to detail and ability to work with complex financial data.</span></li>
<li style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Effective communication and presentation skills, with the ability to convey complex concepts to both technical and non-technical stakeholders.</span></li>
</ul>
<ul style="margin-top:0.0in;margin-bottom:0.0in" type="disc">
<li style="background-color:white;margin-top:0.0in;margin-right:0.0in;margin-bottom:0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Strong technical, analytical and problem solving skills.</span></li>
<li style="background-color:white;margin-top:0.0in;margin-right:0.0in;margin-bottom:0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Must be able to work well under tight deadlines and handle multiple responsibilities.</span></li>
</ul>
<p style="margin:0.0in 2.05pt 0.0in 0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"> </p>
<p style="margin:0.0in 2.05pt 0.0in 0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><strong><u><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Nomura Competencies</span></u></strong></p>
<ul style="margin-bottom:0.0in;margin-top:0.0px">
<li style="margin:0.0in 2.05pt 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><u><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Explore Insights &amp; Vision:</span></u><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"> Identify the underlying causes of problems faced by you or your team and define a clear vision and direction for the future.</span><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"> </span></li>
</ul>
<ul style="margin-bottom:0.0in;margin-top:0.0px">
<li style="margin:0.0in 2.05pt 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><u><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Making Strategic Decisions:</span></u><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"> Evaluate all the options for resolving the problems and effectively prioritize actions or recommendations.</span><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"> </span></li>
<li style="margin:0.0in 2.05pt 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><u><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Inspire Entrepreneurship in People:</span></u><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"> Inspire team members through effective communication of ideas and motivate them to actively enhance productivity.</span><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"> </span></li>
<li style="margin:0.0in 2.05pt 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><u><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Elevate Organizational Capability:</span></u><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"> Engage proactively in professional development and enhance team productivity through the promotion of knowledge sharing. </span><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"> </span></li>
<li style="margin:0.0in 2.05pt 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><u><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Inclusion:</span></u><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"> Respect DEI, foster a culture of psychological safety in the workplace and cultivate a "Risk Culture" (Challenge, Escalate and Respect).</span><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"> </span></li>
</ul>
<p style="margin:0.0in 2.05pt 0.0in 0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"> </p><p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Helvetica N Roman&#39;;color:black"><strong><u><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:windowtext">Diversity Statement</span></u></strong></p>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Helvetica N Roman&#39;;color:black"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:windowtext">Nomura is committed to an employment policy of equal opportunities, and is fundamentally opposed to any less favourable treatment accorded to existing or potential members of staff on the grounds of race, creed, colour, nationality, disability, marital status, pregnancy, gender or sexual orientation. </span></p>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Helvetica N Roman&#39;;color:black"><strong><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:windowtext"> </span></strong></p>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Helvetica N Roman&#39;;color:black"><strong><u><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:windowtext">DISCLAIMER:</span></u></strong><strong><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:windowtext"> </span></strong><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:windowtext">This Job Description is for reference only, and whilst this is intended to be an accurate reflection of the current job, it is not necessarily an exhaustive list of all responsibilities, duties, skills, efforts, requirements or working conditions associated with the job. The management reserves the right to revise the job and may, at his or her discretion, assign or reassign duties and responsibilities to this job at any time. </span></p>

<p style="margin:0.0cm 0.0cm 6.0pt 42.55pt;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"><strong><span style="font-size:12.0pt"> </span></strong></p>
<p style="margin:0.0cm;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"><strong><u><span style="font-size:12.0pt">Company overview</span></u></strong></p>
<p style="margin:0.0cm;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Nomura is a global financial services group with an integrated network spanning approximately 30 countries and regions. By connecting markets East &amp; West, Nomura services the needs of individuals, institutions, corporates and governments through its three business divisions: Wealth Management, Investment Management, and Wholesale (Global Markets and Investment Banking). Founded in 1925, the firm is built on a tradition of disciplined entrepreneurship, serving clients with creative solutions and considered thought leadership. For further information about Nomura, visit <a href="http://www.nomura.com">www.nomura.com</a></p>
<p style="margin:0.0cm;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black"> </p>
<p style="margin:0.0cm;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Department overview:</p>
<p style="margin:0.0cm;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black"> </p>
<p style="margin:0.0cm;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">The Senior Regulatory Reporting VP will play a critical role in supporting Nomura's broader Regulatory Reporting team across EMEA jurisdictions. This position requires a seasoned professional with deep expertise in financial services regulatory frameworks who can navigate complex regulatory changes, perform comprehensive impact assessments, and ensure seamless compliance across multiple jurisdictions while maintaining strong stakeholder relationships.</p>
<p style="margin:0.0cm;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black"> </p>
<p style="margin:0.0cm;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"><strong>Key Responsibilities</strong></p>
<p style="margin:0.0cm;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"><strong>Regulatory Compliance &amp; Analysis</strong></p>
<ul style="margin-bottom:0.0cm;margin-top:0.0px">
<li style="margin:0.0cm 0.0cm 0.0cm 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Engage with Compliance teams on regulatory interpretation for all EMEA jurisdiction regulatory changes and cross-regional impacts affecting EMEA entities</li>
<li style="margin:0.0cm 0.0cm 0.0cm 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Conduct comprehensive gap analysis comparing current reporting practices against post-regulatory change requirements, considering all of Nomura's booking models</li>
<li style="margin:0.0cm 0.0cm 0.0cm 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Perform root cause analyses and impact assessments for regulatory breaches, including cross-jurisdictional analysis</li>
<li style="margin:0.0cm 0.0cm 0.0cm 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Drive remediation plans by partnering with technology teams to design and implement effective solutions for remediation.</li>
<li style="margin:0.0cm 0.0cm 0.0cm 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Ensure procedures, RCSA documentation, and traceability documents are updated following regulatory changes</li>
</ul>
<p style="margin:0.0cm 0.0cm 0.0cm 36.0pt;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"> </p>
<p style="margin:0.0cm 0.0cm 0.0cm 18.0pt;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"><strong>Testing &amp; Quality Assurance</strong></p>
<ul style="margin-bottom:0.0cm;margin-top:0.0px">
<li style="margin:0.0cm 0.0cm 0.0cm 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Coordinate with offshore teams to develop and execute test scenarios/cases for UAT on regulatory changes and remediations</li>
<li style="margin:0.0cm 0.0cm 0.0cm 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Provide oversight of UAT processes and deliver relevant regional sign-offs</li>
<li style="margin:0.0cm 0.0cm 0.0cm 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Conduct post-release checks to ensure implementation effectiveness</li>
</ul>
<p style="margin:0.0cm 0.0cm 0.0cm 36.0pt;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"> </p>
<p style="margin:0.0cm 0.0cm 0.0cm 18.0pt;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"><strong>Project Management &amp; Governance</strong></p>

<p><span style="font-family:arial, helvetica, sans-serif;font-size:10.0pt"><strong><u>Company overview</u></strong></span></p>
<p><span style="font-family:arial, helvetica, sans-serif;font-size:10.0pt">Nomura is a financial services group with an integrated global network. By connecting markets East &amp; West, we service the needs of individuals, institutions, corporates and governments through our four business divisions: Wealth Management, Investment Management, Wholesale (Global Markets and Investment Banking) and Banking.</span><br><br></p>
<p class="MsoBodyTextIndent"><span style="font-family:arial, helvetica, sans-serif;font-size:10.0pt">Driven by the insights of some 28,000 people worldwide, we put our clients at the center of everything we do, delivering unparalleled access to, from and within Asia. For further information about Nomura, visit <a href="http://www.nomura.com">www.nomura.com</a></span></p><p style="font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;MS PGothic&#39;, sans-serif"><span style="font-family:arial, helvetica, sans-serif"><strong><u><span style="font-size:10.0pt"><br>Department Overview:<br><br></span></u></strong><span style="font-size:10.0pt">As the Global Execution Services technology division for Nomura’s Wholesale IT, the group provides brokerage services solutions to clients in over 65 markets around the world.  Through its advanced suite of electronic trading strategies, experienced high-touch trading group, top-ranked Commission Management services, award winning desktop trading platform, and unparalleled access to insightful analytics, content and unique liquidity, Nomura helps institutions lower overall trading costs and ultimately improve investment performance.</span></span></p>
<p style="margin:0.0cm;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Aptos, sans-serif"> </p>
<p style="margin:0.0cm;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Aptos, sans-serif"><span style="font-family:arial, helvetica, sans-serif"><strong><u>Role Description:<br><br></u></strong>Nomura’s proprietary algorithmic trading systems that address our client’s diverse range of trading styles and goals. The platform trades a significant portion of client order flow per day globally and represents a business critical system for the firm.  We provide a core set of global, event-driven, multi asset trading strategies and deploy a library of regularly updated component tactics and extensive controls to refine behavior.  Clients can easily tailor strategies to meet specific objectives.</span><br><br><span style="font-family:arial, helvetica, sans-serif">The team works closely with the Quants to bring new strategies to the market and to constantly evolve the portfolio of the algorithmic offerings of the firm.  The role requires an excellent technologist with deep experience in mission critical and highly evolving systems.</span></p>
<p style="margin:0.0cm 0.0cm 6.0pt;text-indent:0.0cm;font-size:9.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;line-height:12.0pt"> </p>

<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:10.5pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">The Chief Transformation Office (CTO) function is responsible for the delivery of change across the Nomura Group, working closely with the other Chief Corporate Officers in support of the business divisions to ensure we meet our regulatory and strategic long-term goals. Working in Risk transformation gives a great opportunity to understand Nomura Risk businesses, processes and domain. The International Change team partners with business and technology functions to support implementation of various risk solutions. Successful candidates will work closely with other global Risk Officers, Analysts, Risk middle office, Risk Technology, Global Risk Transformation teams and other business functions to deliver various regulatory and strategic risk initiatives.</span></p>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><strong><span style="font-size:12.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"> </span></strong></p>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"> </p>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><strong><span style="font-size:10.5pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Business Overview:</span></strong></p>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:10.5pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">The Risk Management Department at Nomura is broadly organised according to the main risk classes; Credit Risk Management, Market Risk Management, Model Risk and Portfolio Risk.  </span></p>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:10.5pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">The Risk Management team works to provide a firm wide capital management framework, which takes into account the availability and cost of capital, its effective utilization within and across business lines and the overall capital adequacy of the firm. Risk Management's key activities include ensuring transparency and effectively managing market, credit, liquidity, concentration and tail risk within the risk appetite of the firm and monitoring the effectiveness of capital utilization through the use of limit structures, with an emphasis on stress testing and risk adjusted returns on capital efficiency measures.</span></p>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><strong><span style="font-size:10.5pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black"> </span></strong></p>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><strong><span style="font-size:10.5pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">What We Offer:</span></strong></p>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><strong><span style="font-size:10.5pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black"> </span></strong></p>
<ul style="list-style-type:square;margin-bottom:0.0in;margin-top:0.0px">
<li style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">We support employee wellbeing by ensuring a sense of purpose and belonging. </span></li>
<li style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">We offer a comprehensive range of wellbeing services which allows employees to get access to the assistance they need at any point in their wellbeing journey. </span></li>
<li style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Our bespoke benefits support employees and their family’s holistic wellbeing and are inclusive of diverse identities and family structures.</span></li>
</ul>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"> </p>

<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><strong><span style="font-size:10.5pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black"> </span></strong></p>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><strong><span style="font-size:10.5pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Position Specifications:</span></strong></p>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><strong><span style="font-size:10.5pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black"> </span></strong></p>

<p style="text-align:justify;line-height:115%;margin:0.0in;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><strong><span style="font-size:10.5pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black"> </span></strong></p>
<p style="text-align:justify;line-height:115%;margin:0.0in;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><strong><span style="font-size:10.5pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black"> </span></strong></p>
<p style="text-align:justify;line-height:115%;margin:0.0in;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><strong><span style="font-size:10.5pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black"> </span></strong></p>
<p style="text-align:justify;line-height:115%;margin:0.0in;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><strong><span style="font-size:10.0pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Role &amp; Responsibilities:</span></strong></p>
<p style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 6.0pt;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:&#39;Helvetica N Light&#39;"><span style="font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">As part of Risk Management’s Global Book of Work covering Regulatory, Strategic and incremental business change, there is a requirement for a delivery focused project manager with Stress Testing domain knowledge. The candidate would be supporting senior programme/project managers within the Stress Portfolio to deliver across a number of initiatives across Credit Risk. </span></p>
<ul style="margin-bottom:0.0in;margin-top:0.0px">
<li style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Agreeing project scope with stakeholders and managing delivery of project scope throughout the project lifecycle.</span></li>
</ul>
<ul style="margin-bottom:0.0in;margin-top:0.0px" type="disc">
<li style="color:black;margin-top:0.0in;margin-right:0.0in;margin-bottom:0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Supporting resources that form part of the delivery team for the project(s), incorporating all resources and their activities into clearly stated defendable plan for the project(s).</span></li>
<li style="color:black;margin-top:0.0in;margin-right:0.0in;margin-bottom:0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Supporting senior project managers with day to day activities including updating reports and project plans, putting together meeting materials, running project working groups, gathering and distributing working group minutes and coordinating stakeholder discussions.</span></li>
<li style="color:black;margin-top:0.0in;margin-right:0.0in;margin-bottom:0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Helping to define success criteria for each stage of projects and ensuring successful completion of all tasks within the project plan on time and within budget. The overall project will be measured by adherence to delivering against a set of pre-agreed KPIs.</span></li>
<li style="color:black;margin-top:0.0in;margin-right:0.0in;margin-bottom:0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Understanding and monitoring cross-project dependencies that may impact project delivery.</span></li>
<li style="color:black;margin-top:0.0in;margin-right:0.0in;margin-bottom:0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Ensuring delivery of projects is in accordance with Nomura's project management governance protocols and standards.</span></li>
<li style="color:black;margin-top:0.0in;margin-right:0.0in;margin-bottom:0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Effective stakeholder management relating to the project, ensuring through both day to day management, and in governance, that project status clearly articulated and timely in its delivery.</span></li>
<li style="color:black;margin-top:0.0in;margin-right:0.0in;margin-bottom:0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Timely reporting of project status, issues, risks to various management team and governance forum as appropriate</span></li>
<li style="color:black;margin-top:0.0in;margin-right:0.0in;margin-bottom:0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Understanding the front to back risk business processes allowing for optimal requirements gathering and synergies between projects.</span></li>
</ul>
<ul style="margin-bottom:0.0in;margin-top:0.0px">
<li style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Leverage strong foundation of various Stress Testing concepts to manage delivery of business mandatory credit risk related changes</span></li>
<li style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Good communication skills and understanding of key Stress Testing &amp; Credit risk business processes to support presentation of complex topics to senior management.</span></li>
<li style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Act as business gate keeper, control issues being raised to IT and work with the business to ensure issues are raised appropriately and are fully understood/agreed globally. </span></li>
<li style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Works with BAs to assists IT teams with an understanding of requirements and functional requirements specification.</span></li>
<li style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Good team player who would be required to work closely with the global business, IT, BAs, QA and development team throughout project lifecycle.</span></li>
</ul>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"> </p>

<p style="margin:0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><u><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Desired Skills and Experience:</span></u></p>
<p style="margin:0.0in;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"> </p>
<ul style="margin-bottom:0.0in;margin-top:0.0px">
<li style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Equivalent work experience of 3+ years ideally in Stress Testing and Credit Risk management as a Project manager. </span></li>
<li style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Knowledge of the waterfall project management delivery framework</span></li>
<li style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Experience in defining project plans, documenting project RAID, testing approach, test plan and test co-ordination.</span></li>
<li style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Knowledge of key stress testing scenarios, governance and other stress methodologies.</span></li>
<li style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Knowledge of key risk metrics (e.g. PE, EEPE) and other Credit risk methodologies.</span></li>
<li style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Understanding of Stress Testing concepts, stress parameters and data flows.</span></li>
<li style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Understanding of credit risk data flows and architecture including risk limits</span></li>
<li style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Excellent presentational, verbal and written communication skills with the ability to communicate at a range of levels within the organisation.</span></li>
<li style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Exposure to various  tools (e.g. Confluence, JIRA) to manage project deliverables</span></li>
<li style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Strong attention to detail, accuracy and timeliness of delivery.</span></li>
<li style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Detailed knowledge of all Microsoft Office products, i.e. Word, Excel, Project &amp; Power Point</span></li>
<li style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 0.0in 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Management of business analysis resources to support delivery of projects or workstreams</span></li>
</ul>
