<p> </p>
<p><strong>Job title: eTrading Product Manager                     </strong></p>
<p><strong>Corporate Title: Executive Director</strong></p>
<p><strong>Department: Global Markets</strong></p>
<p><strong>Location: London     </strong></p>
<p><strong>                                      </strong></p>
<p><strong>Role Description:</strong></p>
<p>We are seeking a Trading Specialist to join our team in a hybrid role focused on electronic and voice distribution. This position requires deep expertise in electronic trading market structure within the Global Credit space and the ability to work across diverse client segments.</p>
<p> </p>
<p>Key objectives critical to success (and not limited to):</p>
<ul style="list-style-type:disc" type="disc">
<li>Electronic distribution and execution of credit flows via voice and electronic channels.</li>
<li>Monitor and analyze client activity and flow patterns to optimize outcomes.</li>
<li>Provide coverage and support across all client segments in the global credit markets.</li>
<li>Collaborate with sales, trading, and technology teams to enhance electronic trading capabilities.</li>
<li>Ensure compliance with regulatory requirements and internal risk management policies.</li>
</ul>
<p> </p>
<p><strong>Skills, experience, qualifications and knowledge required:</strong></p>
<ul style="list-style-type:disc" type="disc">
<li>Extensive experience with electronic trading market structure in Global Credit markets.</li>
<li>Proven track record in monitoring client activity and managing flows across both electronic and voice.</li>
<li>Advanced proficiency in Microsoft Excel for data analysis and reporting.</li>
<li>Strong organizational skills with attention to detail.</li>
<li>Excellent communication skills for client interaction and internal collaboration.</li>
<li>Ability to work effectively in a fast-paced, dynamic trading environment.</li>
<li>Knowledge of regulatory frameworks governing electronic trading.</li>
<li>Python programming skills for data analysis and automation, highly beneficial.</li>
</ul>
<p> </p>
<p><strong>Work Arrangement</strong></p>
<ul style="list-style-type:disc">
<li>This is a an office-based position.</li>
</ul>
<p> </p>
<p><strong>Nomura Leadership Behaviours </strong></p>
<p><strong>Explore Insights &amp; Vision: </strong>Identify the underlying causes of problems faced by you or your team and define a clear vision and direction for the future.</p>
<p><strong>Making Strategic Decisions: </strong>Evaluate all the options for resolving the problems and effectively prioritize actions or recommendations.</p>
<p><strong>Inspire Entrepreneurship in People</strong>: Inspire team members through effective communication of ideas and motivate them to actively enhance productivity.</p>
<p><strong>Elevate Organizational Capability</strong>: Engage proactively in professional development and enhance team productivity through the promotion of knowledge sharing.</p>
<p><strong>Inclusion: </strong>Respect DEI, foster a culture of psychological safety in the workplace and cultivate a "Risk Culture" (Challenge, Escalate and Respect).</p>
</div>
</div>
<p> </p><p> </p>
<p><strong><u>Right to Work</u></strong><br>The UK Government have taken steps to reduce net migration to the UK by limiting the number of overseas workers coming to the UK for employment. Please note that whilst we are able to consider applications from overseas workers from outside the UK (who require a Tier 2 Skilled Worker visa) we can only employ them if we can provide evidence that this is a genuine vacancy for a qualified role. </p>
<p> </p>
<p><strong><u>Diversity &amp; Inclusion </u></strong></p>
<p>Nomura is an equal opportunity employer. We value diversity and are committed to creating an inclusive environment for all our employees.</p>

<p>If you require any assistance or reasonable adjustments due to a disability or long-term health condition, please do not hesitate to contact us.</p>

<p style="margin:0.0cm 43.55pt 0.3pt 0.0cm;text-align:justify;line-height:113%;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;line-height:113%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Nomura Services, India supports the group’s global businesses. With world-class capabilities in trading support, research, information technology, financial control, operations, risk management and legal support, the firm plays a key role in facilitating the group’s global operations across four international regions. </span></p>
<p style="margin:0.0cm 43.55pt 0.3pt 41.65pt;text-align:justify;line-height:113%;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;line-height:113%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"> </span></p>

<p style="margin:0.0cm 43.55pt 0.3pt 0.0cm;text-align:justify;line-height:113%;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;line-height:113%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">At Nomura, creating an inclusive workplace is a priority. <p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0cm;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><strong><span style="font-size:10.5pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Divisional Overview:</span></strong></p>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0cm;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Global Markets Powai team is an extension of regional Global Markets teams supporting Securitized Products, Rates, FX, Credit and Equities. The division in Powai provides support for a range of activities like research, quantitative modelling, sales and trading support, pricing and balancing, product development and strategy. Powai operation is a critical part of the platform to support the growth of Nomura’s global business.</span></p>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0cm;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"> </p>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0cm;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"> </p>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0cm;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><strong><span style="font-size:10.5pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Business Overview:</span></strong></p>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0cm;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"> </p>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0cm;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Algo Strategies team (Mumbai) is a part of the Global Quants team which is spread across London, New York, Singapore and Mumbai. The Mumbai team consists of people with highly numerate backgrounds such as B.Techs, M.Techs in electrical engineering, computer science engineering, Masters or PhDs in Statistics, Maths, Quantitative Finance and Physics. All members of the team are proficient in some programming language.</span></p>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0cm;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><strong><span style="font-size:10.5pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black"> </span></strong></p>
<p style="margin:0.0cm;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">E-Trading Strategy FX sits within Algo Strategies team in Nomura’s Global Markets Powai division. FX is a strategic franchise for Nomura with the team focusing on all quant and tech elements of the Electronic FX flows within Nomura. This ranges from market making (the models, the curve and subsequent price distribution), our aggregator and the algos within it and other such projects such as our web-enabled Single Dealer Platform. In short – if it’s electronically handled within Nomura it flows through one of the systems the team owns. </span></p>
<p style="margin:0.0cm;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"> </p>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0cm;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">This is a very exciting opportunity to be a key part of this growing team who are focused on building out the Nomura FX franchise. You will be working with the wider development team in Singapore and London to ensure that the products delivered are up to the highest standards to support our 24 hours a day, 5 and half days a week FX business. Your focus will be not only in the quality assurance of the system but also within the reliability. We are committed to engineering systems that are designed to deliver across our franchise with thought in their application.</span></p>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0cm;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><strong><span style="font-size:10.5pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black"> </span></strong></p>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0cm;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"> </p>

Candidates should have interest in number crunching and should have an aptitude for analysing in detail vast amounts of data.</span></p>

<p><strong><span style="font-size:10.5pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Role &amp; Responsibilities</span></strong></p>
<p style="margin:0.0cm 0.0cm 0.0cm 18.0pt;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">We are looking to hire quants </span>to <span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">work on our eFX platform. This position is for talented programmers with experience in trading business and related fields with an inquisitive spirit and extensively contribute to digital transformation of the businesses of the investment bank. </span></p>
<p style="text-align:justify;line-height:115%;margin:0.0cm;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"> </p>
<p style="text-align:justify;text-indent:18.0pt;line-height:115%;margin:0.0cm;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">The role involves -</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top:0.0cm;margin-bottom:6.0pt">
<li style="margin:0.0cm 0.0cm 6.0pt 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Qualitative</span><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"> Assurance of all aspects of FX market making and FX algorithmic trading</span></li>
<li style="margin:0.0cm 0.0cm 6.0pt 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Manage and work on projects related to FX products</span></li>
</ul>
<p style="margin-top:0.0cm;margin-right:0.0cm;margin-bottom:6.0pt;margin-left:0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"> </p>
<p style="text-align:justify;line-height:115%;margin:0.0cm;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><strong><span style="font-size:10.5pt;line-height:115%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Skills, experience, qualifications and knowledge required:</span></strong></p>
<ul style="margin-top:0.0cm;margin-bottom:6.0pt">
<li style="margin:0.0cm 0.0cm 6.0pt 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">5-7 years in financial services QA with minimum 3+ years specifically in FX/trading systems</span></li>
<li style="margin:0.0cm 0.0cm 6.0pt 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Strong technical expertise in Java-based test automation (TestNG, JUnit, Cucumber), API testing, performance testing for low-latency systems, and UI automation (Selenium, Playwright) for React/Vue.js applications</span></li>
<li style="margin:0.0cm 0.0cm 6.0pt 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Preferred FX domain knowledge including FIX protocol testing, regulatory reporting validation, and real-time trading system testing</span></li>
<li style="margin:0.0cm 0.0cm 6.0pt 0.0px;line-height:14.0pt;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Experience of CI / CD pipeline integration (Jenkins, GitLab) with large Gradle build systems would be beneficial</span></li>
<li style="margin:0.0cm 0.0cm 6.0pt 0.0px;line-height:14.0pt;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Exceptional attention to detail with excellent analytical and problem-solving skills, strong communication abilities across technical and business teams, and innovative approach to automation and process improvement</span></li>
<li style="margin:0.0cm 0.0cm 6.0pt 0.0px;line-height:14.0pt;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Familiarity with AI coding assistants and ability to effectively leverage them while maintaining code quality standards</span></li>
<li style="margin:0.0cm 0.0cm 6.0pt 0.0px;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Minimum of a Bachelor's degree in Computer Science from an accredited education institution </span></li>
</ul><p style="margin:0.0cm 43.55pt 0.3pt 0.0cm;text-align:justify;line-height:113%;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><em><span style="font-size:9.0pt;line-height:113%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">We are committed to providing equal opportunities throughout employment including in the recruitment, training and development of employees. We prohibit discrimination in the workplace whether on grounds of gender, marital or domestic partnership status, pregnancy, carer’s responsibilities, sexual orientation, gender identity, gender expression, race, color, national or ethnic origins, religious belief, disability or age.</span></em></p>

<p style="margin:0.0cm -3.2pt 0.3pt 0.0cm;text-align:justify;line-height:113%;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;line-height:113%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Nomura is a financial services group with an integrated global network. By connecting markets East &amp; West, Nomura services the needs of individuals, institutions, corporates and governments through its four business divisions: Wealth Management, Investment Management, Wholesale (Global Markets and Investment Banking), and Banking. <p style="margin:0.0cm -3.2pt 0.3pt 0.0cm;text-align:justify;line-height:113%;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;line-height:113%;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Nomura Services, India supports the group’s global businesses. With world-class capabilities in trading support, research, information technology, financial control, operations, risk management and legal support, the firm plays a key role in facilitating the group’s global operations across four international regions. </span></p>

Over time, we have made meaningful progress in these areas, and this commitment has been well-recognized across the industry. We are proud recipients of the prestigious Top 10 Employers award by the India Workplace Equality Index (IWEI), IWEI Gold Employer of Choice awards, India CSR Leadership Award 2024 for Holistic Village Development Program and the YUVA Unstoppable Changemaker Awards. </span></p>
<p style="margin:0.0cm 21.6pt 0.0cm 0.0cm;text-align:justify;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><strong><span style="font-size:10.5pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Division </span></strong><strong><span style="font-size:10.5pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Overview</span></strong><strong><span style="font-size:10.5pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">:</span></strong></p>
<p style="margin:0.0cm 21.6pt 0.0cm 0.0cm;text-align:justify;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><strong><span style="font-size:10.5pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"> </span></strong></p>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0cm;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Nomura's Global Markets Division handles client transactions for financial institutions, corporates, governments and investment funds around the world. We act as market makers, trading in fixed income and equity securities, including currencies, interest rates and credit in cash, derivatives and structured products. We have taken market-leading positions across the globe by leveraging the strength of our talent, client relationships and technology.</span></p>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0cm;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><strong><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black"> </span></strong></p>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0cm;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><strong><span style="font-size:10.5pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black">Business Overview:</span></strong></p>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0cm;font-size:11.0pt;font-family:Calibri, sans-serif"><strong><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif;color:black"> </span></strong></p>
<p style="margin:0.0cm 7.75pt 0.0cm 0.0cm;text-align:justify;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Global Markets (GM) Powai team is an extension of regional Global Markets (Front Office) teams supporting Equities, Securitized Products, Rates, FX, and Credit. The division in Powai provides support for a range of activities like research, quantitative modelling, sales and trading support, pricing and balancing, product development and strategy. Powai operation is a critical part of the platform to support the growth of Nomura’s global business </span></p>
<p style="margin:0.0cm 0.0cm 0.0cm 6.6pt;text-align:justify;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"> </p>
<p style="margin:0.0cm 7.9pt 0.0cm 0.0cm;text-align:justify;font-size:12.0pt;font-family:&#39;Times New Roman&#39;, serif"><span style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">The Business Management/ CAO team in Powai is a key Business Unit within the GM division. It houses a number of extended teams for various regional functions acting as enablers for the FO team such as AEJ, EMEA &amp; US GM Business Management, Client Account Management and Strategy (CAMS), Front Office Supervision (FOS) and Senior Relationship Management (SRM). </span></p>

<ul style="margin-top:0.0cm;margin-bottom:1.0E-4pt">

<p style="margin:0.0in;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"><strong>Corporate Title</strong>: Vice President</p>
<p style="margin:0.0in;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"><strong>Department:</strong><strong> </strong> Finance</p>
<p style="margin:0.0in;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"><strong>Location:</strong> New York, NY</p>
<p style="margin:0.0in;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"> </p>
<p style="margin:0.0in;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">The pay range for this position at commencement of employment is expected to be between $145K and $175K/year * (see below footnote for additional compensation and benefits information).</p>
<p style="margin:0.0in;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"> </p>
<p style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 8.0pt;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"><strong><u>Company overview</u></strong></p>
<p style="margin:0.0in;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Nomura is a financial services group with an integrated global network. By connecting markets East &amp; West, we service the needs of individuals, institutions, corporates and governments through our four business divisions: Wealth Management, Investment Management, Wholesale (Global Markets and Investment Banking) and Banking.</p>
<p style="margin:0.0in;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"> </p>
<p style="margin:0.0in;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Driven by the insights of some 28,000 people worldwide, we put our clients at the center of everything we do, delivering unparalleled access to, from and within Asia. For further information about Nomura, visit <a href="https://www.nomura.com">www.nomura.com</a> <br><br></p>
<p style="text-align:center;margin:0.0in;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Aon’s Benefit Index®, Nomura’s benefits <strong>rank #1</strong> amongst our competitors</p>
<p style="margin:0.0in;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"> </p>
<p style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 8.0pt;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"><strong><u>Department Overview:</u></strong></p>
<p style="margin:0.0in;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Nomura's Finance department manages the company's financial operations and provides financial guidance to support business decision-making. The team collaborates with senior leadership, business units, and stakeholders to develop effective financial strategies, providing guidance on financial planning, budgeting, forecasting, and analysis to align financial operations with business objectives. Nomura's Finance team specializes in financial reporting, accounting, tax, treasury, and capital management, known for providing exceptional service to clients. With a strong track record and commitment to innovation and global reach, Nomura's Finance department is well-positioned to continue driving growth and success in the financial industry.</p>
<p style="margin:0.0in;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"> </p>
<p style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 6.0pt;text-align:justify;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"> </p>
<p style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 8.0pt;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"><strong><u>Role Description:</u></strong></p>
<p style="margin:0.0in 5.05pt 6.0pt 0.0in;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">This role supports the Equity Derivatives trading desk with a primary focus on Structured Products and Exotic Derivatives, including but not limited to auto callables, barrier options, variance/volatility swaps,  digital/binary options, and structured financing transactions. The ideal candidate will possess a deep understanding of Equity Options, Futures, Swaps, and complex path-dependent and multi-asset derivatives. The successful Product Controller will be highly attentive to detail and risk, demonstrate strong commercial awareness of exotic Greeks and model risk, and uphold the importance of a robust control environment.</p>
<p style="margin:0.0in 5.05pt 6.0pt 0.0in;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"><strong><u>Essential Duties and Responsibilities:</u></strong></p>
<ul style="margin-bottom:0.0in;margin-top:0.0px" type="disc">
<li style="margin-top:0.0in;margin-right:0.0in;margin-bottom:0.0in;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"><strong>Daily P&amp;L Reporting &amp; Analysis:</strong> Flash to Actual analysis, Front-to-Back reconciliations, detailed P&amp;L attribution (including vega, correlation, skew, dividend, and higher-order Greek decomposition for exotic books), verification of manual adjustments, and Balance Sheet Attestation.</li>
<li style="margin-top:0.0in;margin-right:0.0in;margin-bottom:0.0in;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"><strong>Stakeholder Engagement:</strong> Close interaction with the trading desk, Treasury, Middle Office/Ops, IT, Market Risk, Financial Reporting, Regulatory, and IPV teams to resolve booking, valuation, and financial resource usage-related issues — with particular focus on complex lifecycle events (barriers, autocall triggers, put financing transactions)</li>
<li style="margin-top:0.0in;margin-right:0.0in;margin-bottom:0.0in;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"><strong>Process Improvement:</strong> Input into the development of tactical and strategic process improvement projects, including automation of exotic P&amp;L explain workflows, as well as testing and implementation of end-state solutions</li>
<li style="margin-top:0.0in;margin-right:0.0in;margin-bottom:0.0in;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"><strong>Funding &amp; Financing:</strong> Understanding daily summary of funding charges, including day-over-day variance analysis and the impact of structured product collateral/margin dynamics.</li>
<li style="margin-top:0.0in;margin-right:0.0in;margin-bottom:0.0in;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"><strong>Manual Adjustments:</strong> Understanding, challenge, and motivation to resolve all manual adjustments, including model reserves, Day 1 P&amp;L, and valuation adjustments specific to illiquid exotics</li>
<li style="margin-top:0.0in;margin-right:0.0in;margin-bottom:0.0in;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"><strong>Team Leadership:</strong> Managing junior team members both onshore and offshore; mentoring on exotic product knowledge and control best practices.</li>
<li style="margin-top:0.0in;margin-right:0.0in;margin-bottom:0.0in;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"><strong>Deal Review &amp; Structuring Support:</strong> Deal review analysis and return, as well as review of deal reviews performed by the offshore team</li>
<li style="margin-top:0.0in;margin-right:0.0in;margin-bottom:0.0in;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"><strong>Controls &amp; KRIs:</strong> Ensuring proper controls are in place as measured by KRI metrics, with specific attention to model risk, market data quality, and lifecycle event processing for exotic structures</li>
<li style="margin-top:0.0in;margin-right:0.0in;margin-bottom:0.0in;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"><strong>Month-End Close:</strong> Closing of the firm's books and records, including required manual journal entries and Balance Sheet substantiation; reconciliation of monthly FO P&amp;L to General Ledger; completion of various financial reporting schedules; reserve and Day 1 P&amp;L amortization reviews</li>
</ul>
<p style="margin:0.0in;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"> </p>
<p style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 8.0pt;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"><strong><u>Skills, experience, qualifications and knowledge required:</u></strong></p>
<ul style="margin-bottom:0.0in;margin-top:0.0px" type="disc">
<li style="color:#333333;margin-top:0.0in;margin-right:0.0in;margin-bottom:0.0in;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">At least 8–10 years of Equities-focused Product Control experience, with a minimum of 4–5 years covering Structured Products and/or Exotic Derivatives (autocallables, barriers, variance swaps, correlation products, bespoke payoffs)</li>
<li style="color:#333333;margin-top:0.0in;margin-right:0.0in;margin-bottom:0.0in;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Strong understanding of exotic derivative pricing models, Greeks (including cross-Greeks for multi-asset products), and associated model risk</li>
<li style="color:#333333;margin-top:0.0in;margin-right:0.0in;margin-bottom:0.0in;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Familiarity with lifecycle events unique to structured/exotic products (barrier breaches, autocall observations, early termination, dividend adjustments)</li>
<li style="color:#333333;margin-top:0.0in;margin-right:0.0in;margin-bottom:0.0in;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">BS/BA in Finance, Economics, Accounting, or a quantitative discipline; CFA, CPA, or FRM designation preferred</li>
<li style="color:#333333;margin-top:0.0in;margin-right:0.0in;margin-bottom:0.0in;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Proven ability to manage and develop junior staff in a global team structure</li>
<li style="color:#333333;margin-top:0.0in;margin-right:0.0in;margin-bottom:0.0in;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Ability to multitask in a fast-paced, results-oriented environment with strong prioritization skills</li>
<li style="color:#333333;margin-top:0.0in;margin-right:0.0in;margin-bottom:0.0in;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Strong verbal and written communication skills, with the ability to articulate complex product P&amp;L drivers to senior stakeholders</li>
<li style="color:#333333;margin-top:0.0in;margin-right:0.0in;margin-bottom:0.0in;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif">Team player, proactive, self-starter who can operate dynamically while maintaining rigorous attention to detail</li>
</ul>
<p style="margin:0.0in;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"> </p>
<p style="margin:0.0in 0.0in 8.0pt;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"><strong><u>Nomura Leadership Behaviours</u></strong></p>
<p style="text-align:justify;margin:0.0in;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"> </p>
<ul style="margin-top:0.0in;margin-bottom:0.0in" type="disc">
<li style="margin-top:0.0in;margin-right:0.0in;margin-bottom:0.0in;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"><strong>Explore Insights &amp; Vision: </strong>Identify the underlying causes of problems faced by you or your team and define a clear vision and direction for the future.</li>
<li style="margin-top:0.0in;margin-right:0.0in;margin-bottom:0.0in;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"><strong>Making Strategic Decisions</strong>: Evaluate all the options for resolving the problems and effectively prioritize actions or recommendations.</li>
<li style="margin-top:0.0in;margin-right:0.0in;margin-bottom:0.0in;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"><strong>Inspire Entrepreneurship in People</strong>: Inspire team members through effective communication of ideas and motivate them to actively enhance productivity.</li>
<li style="margin-top:0.0in;margin-right:0.0in;margin-bottom:0.0in;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"><strong>Elevate Organizational Capability</strong>: Engage proactively in professional development and enhance team productivity through the promotion of knowledge sharing.</li>
<li style="margin-top:0.0in;margin-right:0.0in;margin-bottom:0.0in;font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Arial, sans-serif"><strong>Inclusion</strong>: Foster a culture of inclusion and psychological safety in the workplace and cultivate a "Risk Culture" (Challenge, Escalate and Respect).</li>
</ul>

<p><strong>Divisional Overview:</strong></p>
<p>The Operations division provides the support and control infrastructure that enables the Firm to develop and deliver financial products throughout the world. Working with the Sales, Trading and Information Technology Divisions, Operations professionals are responsible for the management and execution of transactions in a manner which maximizes profitability and minimizes risk. Our division is responsible for the management and execution of millions of securities transactions on a daily basis. Our responsibilities include trade processing, confirmation, settlement, fails management, margin, asset servicing, risk management, reference data management and various other client services. In partnership with sales, trading, prime services, information technology and other teams from across the bank, we apply a continual focus on improving productivity and efficiency, while seeking new and innovative ways to support our clients' changing needs. </p>
<p> </p>
<p><strong>Business Overview:</strong></p>
<p>The KYC team in Powai is part of the CSG function and plays an extremely key role in the firm supporting AEJ, Japan, EMEA and US businesses with a current team size of 130+ team members which includes 8-10 Associates / Managers. The team supports KYC On-boarding &amp; Rolling Reviews for low, medium and high-risk clients, including private clients. Along with KYC, the responsibility of the team is also to provide solution on the potential issues identified by the various stakeholders in the firm and provide a turnaround time to be able to resolve the issues associated to KYC. The team works with the business and acts as an AML/KYC governance enabling function ensuring that all relevant AML/ KYC risks and regulations are properly managed. The team also closely works with the regional partners on audit and data quality associated tasks. There are plans to expand Powai footprint and develop / leverage Powai capability by transitioning more activities over to the Powai KYC team.</p>
